# Football Shorts & Videos — frame-by-frame report (2026-11-20)

Data: `football_shorts.db` (SQLite, MySQL-compatible schema). 110 Shorts + 110 long videos. Frames every 2 s; long videos analysed on the **first 60 s only**. Frame text descriptions are model-written from contact sheets: treat tile alignment as ±1–3 frames in dense videos; people are named only from titles/captions/overlays, not faces.
